Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - Continue structural cleanup from previous session (facade files moving into WGP)
   - Analyze `debug_20260212_192125.log` to understand why qwen_image_style tasks took so long between runs
   - Fix the root cause of ~7 minute delays (LoRA unload/reload on every generation)
   - Audit whether LoRA handling is standardized and well-structured across the codebase
   - Plan a comprehensive cleanup to standardize ALL LoRA handling, with special attention to phase config complexity

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **WGP LoRA lifecycle**: `offload.load_loras_into_model()` (mmgp) loads LoRA weights from disk into transformer model; `offload.unload_loras_from_model()` clears all LoRA state. WGP calls unload after EVERY generation (wgp.py:6439), causing ~7 min reload per task for 20B models with CPU offloading
   - **mmgp offload module**: From `mmgp` package (v3.5.6). `load_loras_into_model` internally calls `unload_loras_from_model` before loading — so even without explicit unload, reloading always happens
   - **Phase config**: Multi-phase generation (2 or 3 phases) where LoRA multipliers vary per phase. Format: semicolon-separated values `"1.0;0.5"` (phase 1=1.0, phase 2=0.5). Space-separated between LoRAs: `"1.0;0.5 0.8;1.2"`
   - **Hires fix two-pass**: Qwen handler creates `hires_config` with `phase_lora_multipliers` for two-pass generation. Only pass 1 values sent to WGP; pass 2 managed by qwen_main.py
   - **Lightning LoRA detection**: `is_lightning_lora()` in phase_multiplier_utils.py detects by filename patterns. Lightning LoRAs typically disabled in phase 2 (`"0.9;0"`)
   - **LoRAConfig typed system**: `LoRAEntry` dataclass with url/local_path/filename/multiplier/status fields. `LoRAConfig` with from_params(), from_phase_config(), from_segment_loras(), to_wgp_format(), merge() methods. Well-designed but not consistently used across codebase
   - **SVI (Style-Video-Inspired) LoRAs**: Predefined LoRA URLs in `travel/svi_config.py` with phase multipliers like `"1.2;0"` and `"0;1.1"`, merged via raw array manipulation

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py`** — WGP monkey-patches for headless operation
     - Added `apply_lora_caching_patch()` function (lines 287-366) to cache LoRA loading between generations
     - Registered in `apply_all_wgp_patches()` as `results["lora_caching"]`
     - Key implementation:
     ```python
     def apply_lora_caching_patch() -> bool:
         from mmgp import offload
         _original_load = offload.load_loras_into_model
         _original_unload = offload.unload_loras_from_model

         def _cached_load(model, lora_path, lora_multi=None, **kwargs):
             if kwargs.get("check_only", False):
                 return _original_load(model, lora_path, lora_multi, **kwargs)
             paths = lora_path if isinstance(lora_path, list) else [lora_path]
             requested = tuple(paths)
             cached = getattr(model, "_headless_cached_lora_paths", None)
             if (cached is not None and cached == requested
                     and getattr(model, "_loras_adapters", None) is not None):
                 model_logger.essential(f"[LORA_CACHE] Skipping LoRA reload — same {len(requested)} LoRAs already loaded")
                 return
             if cached is not None:
                 model_logger.essential(f"[LORA_CACHE] LoRAs changed ({len(cached)} -> {len(requested)}), reloading")
             else:
                 model_logger.essential(f"[LORA_CACHE] First LoRA load: {len(requested)} LoRAs")
             _original_unload(model)
             result = _original_load(model, lora_path, lora_multi, **kwargs)
             model._headless_cached_lora_paths = requested
             return result

         def _deferred_unload(model):
             if model is None:
                 return
             model_logger.debug("[LORA_CACHE] Deferring LoRA unload (cached for reuse)")

         offload.load_loras_into_model = _cached_load
         offload.unload_loras_from_model = _deferred_unload
         return True
     ```

> AGENT

The investigation confirms there's no functional reason for either the double resolution or the SVI double-merge. Let me update the plan with the analysis.

**Double LoRA resolution findings:**
- `apply_phase_config_patch()` does NOT reference LoRA paths — it only patches model architecture (guidance_phases, guidance_scale, flow_shift, solver)
- The travel handler **doesn't pass** `_parsed_phase_config` through to `generation_params` (unlike `task_conversion.py` which does). This is an architectural inconsistency
- The queue worker (`convert_to_wgp_task_impl()`) defensively re-parses everything from scratch because it's designed to be idempotent
- No code between LoRA download and GenerationTask submission needs resolved paths

**SVI double-merge findings:**
- `segment_payload["lora_names"]` (set by orchestrator) is NEVER read by the segment handler — confirmed by grep
- The segment handler builds generation_params from scratch, only populating LoRAs from segment_loras, phase_config, or SVI merge
- The orchestrator merge uses raw URLs while the segment handler merge uses resolved paths (different inputs), but since the orchestrator result is never consumed, this doesn't matter

**Root cause:** Both patterns are historical accumulation — the queue worker was built as a self-contained generic path, the travel handler was built as a sequential flow, and they were never reconciled.
